# Skills · C · part 189

877 entries, collision-cross-section-regression to coloring-page. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **collision-cross-section-regression**: Use when experimental CCS values for small molecules must be predicted or modeled from structures (e.g. AllCCS/BushCCS data), especially to test whether an MS/MS-pretrained encoder improves CCS regression over training from scratch. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/collision-cross-section-regression))
- **collision-cross-section-trajectory-calculation**: Use when gas-phase ion geometries and their DFT atomic charges are available and a CCS value is needed for comparison to TWIMS measurements or metabolite annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/collision-cross-section-trajectory-calculation))
- **collision-energy-calibration**: Use when search-engine results and spectra (raw/mzml/d/hdf) are available and you need the per-file optimal NCE for Prosit/Koina spectral predictions, e.g. before spectral library generation or rescoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/collision-energy-calibration))
- **collision-energy-condition-comparison**: Use when evaluating whether in silico predicted spectra can rank the true compound from a candidate list filtered by precursor mass, and when comparing ranking accuracy across single versus merged collision-energy conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/collision-energy-condition-comparison))
- **collision-energy-matched-fragment-annotation**: Use when both CFM-ID-predicted fragment lists and experimental HRMS/MS spectra (multi collision energy, e.g. 20/40/60 eV) exist for DNA adduct standards, and annotation confidence from fragment overlap is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/collision-energy-matched-fragment-annotation))
- **collision-energy-matching-and-stepped-spectrum-merging**: Use when evaluating simulated MS/MS spectra against challenge or reference sets whose acquisition used stepped collision energies or multiple CE replicate measurements, so predicted and experimental spectra must be compared on a consistent collision-energy basis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/collision-energy-matching-and-stepped-spectrum-merging))
- **collision-energy-metadata-curation**: Use when preparing MS/MS training data from spectral libraries (MoNA, NIST 17) where spectra for the same compound were acquired with heterogeneous collision energies or instrument types, and you need energy-aware filtering, merging, or train/test splits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/collision-energy-metadata-curation))
- **collision-energy-model-activation**: Use when building PRM/MRM transition lists in LipidCreator where fragment-specific collision energies must be predicted by the flipR nonlinear regression model (minpack.lm / nls.multstart) and exported with the transition list rather than hand-tuned per precursor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/collision-energy-model-activation))
- **collision-energy-ms2-spectra-handling**: Use when comparing user-acquired tissue MS2 spectra from mzML files against Met-ID's FMP-10-derivatized standards MS2 database, and you need quantitative cosine-similarity scores under explicit bin-size choices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/collision-energy-ms2-spectra-handling))
- **collision-energy-optimization-configuration**: Use when building a Skyline small-molecule transition list (MRM/SRM targets with heavy-labeled internal standards) whose exported instrument method needs per-transition predicted collision energies rather than fixed defaults. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/collision-energy-optimization-configuration))
- **collision-energy-optimization-for-fragmentation**: Use when when you have N-Me derivatized unsaturated sterol lipid structures (as SMILES or molecular formula) and need to predict MS/MS fragmentation patterns with collision-energy-dependent m/z values and intensities for downstream CCS prediction or LC-IM-MS/MS library matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/collision-energy-optimization-for-fragmentation) · 3 copies)
- **collision-energy-resolved-msms-comparison**: Use when you have isomer-pair MS/MS spectra acquired at multiple collision energies and need sensitivity/specificity of conventional single-spectrum similarity matching per NCE condition as a baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/collision-energy-resolved-msms-comparison))
- **collision-energy-response-curve-fitting**: Use when DDA LC-MS/MS mzML files contain scans of the same precursor at multiple NCE steps and under both CID and HCD, and you need to compare product ion signal (e.g., m/z 85.0824, 10 ppm) versus NCE between fragmentation modes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/collision-energy-response-curve-fitting))
- **collision-energy-response-quality-assessment**: Use when fitted flipR log-normal dissociation profiles over QExactive HF NCE 10-60 or QTOF CE 10-100 ranges need a fit-quality audit (relative residuals, coverage statistics) before selecting optimal CEs for a targeted lipidomics assay. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/collision-energy-response-quality-assessment))
- **collision-energy-spectra-consolidation**: Use when preparing enhanced MS/MS training or query inputs from MSP-derived spectra where each compound has multiple MS2 scans at different collision energies, and a single binarized merged spectrum with neutral losses is needed for model input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/collision-energy-spectra-consolidation))
- **collision-energy-spectra-merging**: Use when LC-MS/MS data contain multiple fragmentation spectra per feature at different collision energies (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/collision-energy-spectra-merging))
- **collision-energy-spectra-organization**: Use when experimental MS/MS spectra of reference standards (e.g., the 15 DNA adduct standards) have been acquired at multiple collision energies and fragments must be organized per standard and energy into a searchable fragment datatable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/collision-energy-spectra-organization))
- **collision-energy-spectra-pairing**: Use when raw tandem MS/MS peak lists (m/z and intensity CSVs) must be converted into embeddings for molecular structure recommendation with Spec2Mol, and the acquisition provides paired [M+H]+/[M-H]- spectra at low (35% NCE) and high (130% NCE) collision energies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/collision-energy-spectra-pairing))
- **collision-energy-spectral-aggregation**: Use when you have multiple ESI-MS2 record files (MassBank record format with accession ID, peaks, collision-energy annotation) of the same compound at different CID energies and need one merged reference spectrum per compound. - **collision-energy-spectrum-handling**: Use when a feature table already has isotope-mapped entries and measured MS2 spectra (e.g. MS2data_example.csv) plus an MS2 database (DBMS2_test_pos.csv) are available, and you want to score candidate annotations by fragmentation pattern similarity. - **collision-energy-spectrum-merging**: Use when multiple MS/MS spectra of the same compound (same InChIKey) were acquired at different collision energies and must be consolidated into a single spectrum per compound for downstream fingerprint prediction or spectral feature table construction.
